| 462 | medium | finding: token-alias | Unit-token aliasing: when a mob targets me, targettarget (and often nameplate/softenemy) refers to the same unit as player, so every player signal is duplicated on those tokens. Mining collapses aliases to a canonical token before scoring; this row reports the aliasing itself. | status cross-run, alreadyLive False, support None/None, prec None, lift None, captures run16 |  | Offline: canonicalize tokens via same-frame payload identity + Ledger token→GUID before mining. Live: Ledger already caches token→GUID; avoid double-counting aliased tokens in attribution. |

## Miss explainer

| reason | n | what would fix it |
|---|---|---|
| not-on-tracked-token | 350 | track the unit on nameplate/softenemy tokens through the Identity ledger (token->GUID) and feed it to the HSM |
| no-api-signal | 121 | engine limit: no live event carries this (use C_DamageMeter totals after combat, or accept the gap) |
| secret-payload | 60 | signal arrives but its payload is secret: resolve through the Ledger (cached clear value) or elimination |
| target-changed | 50 | keep the previous target GUID for 0.4 s after PLAYER_TARGET_CHANGED (ledger 'token' entry) and match against both |
| wrong-attribution | 23 | attributed to the wrong source: tighten the rule (cast-frame / DM per-source) for this category |
| concurrent-source | 17 | another source hit the same unit in the same window: use cast-frame evidence or the DM per-source delta |
| timing-window | 15 | the live signal exists but outside the matching window: widen the rule window (see the observed lag) |
| recorder-throttle | 13 | the HSM had it, the offline recorder dropped it (5/s cap): raise RecordDB cap for this event or rely on CombatLogDB |
| amount-mismatch | 1 | a live signal is in the window with a different amount (partial absorb/resist/overkill): match with tolerance |

## v2.2.4 semantic lint + reality cross-check (standing check list)

Every build: stored descriptions re-parsed with the current parser; rules: lockout/interrupt text never becomes auraDuration; summon lifetimes/pulses and imbue durations never become aura or cast amounts; every 'N sec/min' is classified (aura / lockout / stun / summon / imbue / period / header) or counted ambiguous; multi-cost spells keep every cost; tooltip header fields agree with the structured APIs; a parsed aura duration never observed is 'unverified'.
